Directory Structure

Collapsible directory tree showing file and symbol counts. Directories with LLM summaries show them inline.

java/5 files · 20 symbols
This Java directory contains utilities for processing BMDExpress 3 benchmark dose (BMD) analysis data, including exporters for genomics results, category analyses, and integrated projects in JSON/TSV formats. It also includes a statistical prefiltering tool that performs Williams trend tests and Dunnett's comparisons on dose-response data using the same libraries as BMDExpress itself. Together, these tools enable extracting, integrating, and analyzing BMD results across multiple project files and experimental conditions.
output/1 files · 1 symbols
The `output` directory contains a Python script that generates formatted Word documents describing a toxicogenomics interpretation pipeline. This pipeline processes gene expression data through multiple analysis stages to produce comprehensive, literature-grounded reports for scientific research or clinical applications.
pdf_text/3 files · 51 symbols
A lightweight, zero-dependency PDF parsing library that extracts semantic text from PDFs by resolving object graphs, decoding compressed streams, and applying structure tags from marked-content operators. It includes comprehensive support for ToUnicode CMaps, font encodings, and table detection, plus a lossless codec that can decompose PDFs into labeled byte spans and reconstruct them byte-perfectly.
tests/25 files · 353 symbols
The tests directory contains the test suite for the project with pytest configuration and fixtures. conftest.py provides isolated testing infrastructure including state management (patched filesystem), a FastAPI test client, fixture data, and mocks for external dependencies like Java subprocesses, HTTP requests, and the Anthropic API. This setup enables unit and integration testing of the codebase in a controlled environment.
e2e/4 files · 18 symbols
tests/e2e contains end-to-end tests for a data pipeline system using Playwright, validating the complete flow from session loading through validation, integration, processing, and approval. It provides shared testing fixtures, debugging utilities with screenshots and manual inspection checkpoints, and smoke tests using golden session data to verify pipeline correctness.
fixtures/1 files · 0 symbols
The tests/fixtures directory is a Python package that serves as a container for test fixtures and test data. Currently, it contains only an empty `__init__.py` file, making it available as an importable package for the test suite.
integration/6 files · 45 symbols
Integration tests for a FastAPI backend system that manages chemical/BMD data workflows, covering file upload endpoints, pool validation and integration for merging multiple data sources, session lifecycle management with version control, and processing integrated data with domain model validation across multiple platforms.
unit/12 files · 279 symbols
This directory contains a comprehensive unit test suite for a scientific data processing and reporting system, covering core domain logic like BMD project schema validation, document tree structure, footnote management, and data integration, as well as output generation modules for HTML and LaTeX export, partitioning and filtering, and session persistence. The tests verify functional correctness of utility functions, schema constraints, data transformations, and file I/O operations across the codebase.

File Inventory

FileLanguageLinesSymbols
abstract_apical.pypython4757
Generates deterministic narrative text for apical BMD (benchmark dose) findings in toxicology reports, including per-platform body-Results narratives and Abstract summaries without LLM calls.
abstract_genomics.pypython2753
Builds deterministic prose for the genomics section of scientific abstracts, describing the most sensitive gene sets and genes per organ and sex with their BMD/BMDL values.
abstract_methods.pypython1561
Builds the Abstract → Methods paragraph of a report deterministically from MethodsContext by populating a structured template with study data (compound name, doses, species, route, duration, organs, BMR) without requiring an LLM call.
abstract_pk.pypython1554
Builds a deterministic Abstract→Results paragraph summarizing plasma concentration measurements and calculated half-lives from pharmacokinetic biosampling data, comparing results across animal sexes.
abstract_summary.pypython3155
Aggregator module that constructs Abstract Summary and Results paragraphs by combining apical endpoint, genomics, and pharmacokinetic findings into NIEHS-formatted report text.
apical_bmds.pypython3294
Runs EPA BMDS (pybmds) continuous models on apical endpoint dose-response data to produce benchmark dose estimates, confidence limits, and NIEHS classification bins—providing an alternative summary to BMDExpress 3's native results using EPA-recommended model selection.
background_server.pypython4258
FastAPI application shell for 5dToxReport that configures the app, applies middleware (user authentication, no-cache), mounts endpoint routers, and serves the web UI.
background_writer.pypython76610
LLM-based background/introduction section writer for toxicology studies that transforms gathered chemical and regulatory data into formal 6-7 paragraph scientific prose using Claude API (primary) or Ollama (fallback).
bmd_project_schema.pypython99319
Pydantic models that validate `integrated.json` files from bmdx-pipe, acting as a runtime load-time barrier to catch schema drift and enforce data integrity before consumption by downstream code. Includes deduplication logic for legacy apical experiments and lossless preservation of unknown fields via `extra="allow"`.
body_weight_table.pypython6983
Builds NIEHS Table 2 (Body Weights) from sidecar JSON files, computing mean±SE directly from raw Core Animal data with proper attrition tracking and BMD result integration.
build_db.pypython40810
Builds bmdx.duckdb, a normalized DuckDB analytical database integrating papers, genes, GO terms, pathways, and citation networks from JSON and TSV source files.
build_docx.pypython1106
Provides reusable utility functions for formatting python-docx Document objects with styled headings, paragraphs, bullet lists, and tables.
cache_plumbing.pypython44011
Implements per-section disk caching, hash-based cache invalidation, and serialization helpers for an expensive biomedical data processing pipeline. Each cache unit (NTP, BMDS, genomics, etc.) has a dedicated hash function that invalidates only when relevant inputs change, plus schema versions to force misses on output format changes.
chem_resolver.pypython49713
Resolves chemical identifiers (names, CASRN, CID, DTXSID, EC numbers) to comprehensive metadata by querying PubChem PUG REST and EPA CompTox Chemicals Dashboard APIs in a two-pass resolution strategy.
citegraph.pypython88328
Citation graph crawler using Semantic Scholar API to expand research networks from seed papers with governor-controlled stopping based on relevance scoring, saturation detection, and budget limits; supports organ-specific toxicogenomics crawls.
clinical_pathology_table.pypython3961
Builds NIEHS clinical pathology summary tables (Tables 4/5/6) by combining raw animal-level Core Animal counts from sidecar JSON with NTP statistical analysis results, producing formatted tables with endpoint rows, significance markers, and BMD/BMDL values organized by sex.
crawl_phase2.pypython4703
Phase 2 literature crawl orchestrator that runs targeted searches across mechanism-specific (NRF2, inflammasome, cell death, circadian, senescence), organ-gap (intestine, testis, vascular), and gene-toxicity domains to expand a toxicology knowledge base.
data_gatherer.pypython111319
A regulatory and toxicological data gatherer that queries multiple public databases (EPA CTX, PubChem, ATSDR, IRIS, OSHA/NIOSH, SDWA, Semantic Scholar, CPDat) to collect raw data for writing structured toxicology background sections for chemicals.
document_tree.pypython60711
Defines the complete document structure of an NIEHS 5-day biological potency report as a tree of nodes, serving as the single source of truth for heading hierarchy, section ordering, table numbering, and TOC generation. Provides utilities to query, traverse, and serialize the tree for rendering and navigation.
enrichr_analysis.pypython2173
CLI tool for running Enrichr enrichment analysis on genes extracted from rlm-bmdx genomics session files. Handles argument parsing, session file reading, and results output formatting while delegating API calls to enrichr_client.py.
enrichr_client.pypython2143
Minimal client for the Enrichr gene set enrichment analysis REST API, providing functions to submit gene lists, fetch enrichment results, and perform end-to-end enrichment analysis against multiple libraries with zero external dependencies.
export_routes.pypython3216
Provides FastAPI routes for exporting 5dToxReport documents in multiple formats (Overleaf LaTeX bundle, HTML preview, .bm2 enriched file) and managing a global writing style profile that the LLM uses to match user preferences.
export_sessions.pypython2366
Exports Claude Code session JSONL transcripts to readable Markdown files, formatting messages, tool interactions, and results with collapsible long outputs.

LLM-based extraction system that uses local Ollama instances in parallel to extract scientific metadata (genes, organs, claims, methods) from paper abstracts and full texts, with gene consensus analysis and normalization.

Runner script that executes Phase 2 extraction across 9 research topic directories using ParallelExtractionEngine, processing papers from JSON files and saving extractions with timing metrics.

pdf_text/parse_pdf.pypython218742
A from-scratch PDF parser that resolves the object graph, decompresses Object Streams, walks the page tree, tokenizes content streams, and extracts text chunks classified by semantic role using marked-content tags.
pdf_text/pdf_codec.pypython4309
Lossless PDF decomposer that splits files into labeled byte spans (header, objects, gaps, trailer) stored in JSON with base64 encoding, and reassembler that reconstructs the original file byte-perfectly with SHA-256 verification.

Superuser tool for managing the 5dToxReport file pool, providing commands to list files by platform, delete files for specific platforms, and invalidate downstream artifacts.
pool_fingerprints.pypython3696
Manages file-pool fingerprinting and lightweight validation by extracting structural metadata from uploaded files, storing fingerprints in memory and on disk, and validating new files against existing pools. Automatically converts long-format CSV/txt files to wide format and supports session restore by caching and reloading fingerprints.
pool_globals.pypython1435
Module holding shared mutable state (in-memory dicts), a FastAPI router, and path helpers for the pool-orchestrator subsystem; designed to break import cycles in split pool modules.
pool_orchestrator.pypython1860
Backward-compatible facade that re-exports public APIs from nine split submodules, allowing existing consumers to import from pool_orchestrator without changes. Also serves as the module-load trigger point for route decorators from pool_routes, integrated_io, and process_integrated.
pool_routes.pypython4805
File-pool lifecycle route handlers implementing the upload → validate → confirm-metadata → integrate flow with four POST endpoints that drive validation, conflict resolution, metadata confirmation, and file merging.
pool_state.pypython2023
Handles pool-state mutations for data validation sessions: checks progression status, removes stale file entries during re-uploads, and invalidates downstream artifacts while preserving BMDS caches and user narrative edits.
process_integrated.pypython9567
Owns two heavy POST handlers that orchestrate complex processing pipelines: api_process_integrated is the "god function" that orchestrates NTP statistics, BMDS modeling, section cards, LLM narratives, and genomics extraction with disk caching; api_generate_animal_report generates per-animal traceability reports from fingerprinted files.
processing_helpers.pypython10138
Pipeline helper functions that transform integrated BMD project data through multi-stage processing: filtering gene expression, partitioning by platform, building serialized section cards, extracting genomics and adversity signatures, and generating BMD summary tables for toxicology reports.
render_capabilities.pypython1564
Maps document semantic node types to their rendering capabilities (orientation, page breaks, editability), serving as the single coupling point between document template and interactive rendering layer. Provides a decoupled dictionary so template evolution doesn't require renderer changes, and annotates serialized trees with capabilities for the frontend.

Central module for managing shared in-memory server state across FastAPI route handlers, providing typed accessors to bm2 and CSV upload stores to avoid circular imports.
session_routes.pypython158016
FastAPI route handlers for session persistence, approval workflow, and version history management. Provides endpoints for loading/saving/approving report sections, managing experiment metadata, restoring past versions, and destructively resetting session or data pool state.
